# Panel review of the Nature Communications manuscript — 6 September 2026

Independent 18-reviewer panel (sections, figures, supplement, statistics, methods, prior work, release) over a frozen snapshot of the working tree taken 2026-09-05 22:33 (HEAD 560d5f2 plus the uncommitted 5 September revision; main.tex last edited 22:42). 158 merged findings; every medium/high finding was checked by an independent skeptical verifier who re-read the cited lines and recomputed numbers from source_data and scripts, high findings by two. Verifier verdicts: {'partially-confirmed': 78, 'confirmed': 39, 'refuted': 1}; refuted: 0. Findings are listed by verified severity; where a verifier corrected a claim the corrected wording is shown. Low-severity polish items were not verified. A completeness critic then looked for what the panel missed; its findings are appended unverified. No manuscript file was modified by the panel. Agents: 139; tokens: 1.68M; wall time: 51 min plus the interrupted first run.

## Editor synthesis

This is a numerically impeccable, unusually honest manuscript that does not yet know what it is about. Across eighteen reviewers every headline number recomputed from Source Data matched (MNIST ladder, K=3 capture, the 1.27-pp ancestry contrast, calibration regrets, 1,407/1,680 bound attainment, 85%/7% anatomical efficiency, focal-shunt calibration contrasts, the measured-response oracle); the theory in S1-S4 and the centered-cut proposition are correct; every prespecification seal, exclusion log and failed gate is retained; nothing was refuted. The real contribution is narrower and stronger than the title and abstract claim: (i) the exact E/I gradient factorization and the route-dictionary/capture language (main.tex 84-131); (ii) the cut-rank theorem, dynamic program and estimate-then-learn pipeline of R7/Fig 6, which is a forward-representability result about input grouping rather than a feedback-dictionary result; and (iii) three well-controlled boundary results (one signal per neuron suffices on images; a focal shunt is a diagonal gain on the ancestry-route partition whose selectivity, not magnitude, depends on conductance state; measured responses show no endogenous route use). The biggest scientific risk is that three biological sections currently state conclusions the authors' own data do not support as worded: the depth-bin-over-subtree ordering in Fig 7C is an artifact of the K=1 depth bin being the soma broadcast at 96% of the rank-1 oracle (M069); the Fig 8 'electrotonic boundary' is a loss of selectivity rather than of effect, measured while the absolute dose covaries 50-fold (M011-M013); and the Fig 9 'subtree' null tests mostly single-site distal addresses covering ~48% of sites against a field that is 98% a fixed transfer profile (M014, M015). The biggest editorial risk is that at ~8,300 main-text words, ~8,000 Methods words, ten equal-weight Results sections and a Results-order Discussion, referees will not be able to name the advance (M001, M002, M016, M017). A third high-severity cluster is disclosure: the predecessor preprint is uncited in the main text (M003), every aligned-D3 physical-depth fit ran to the epoch cap with validation loss still falling (M007), the active-channel ensemble contributes ~1% of the operator (M013), and the 'synthetic noise-resilience task' is undefined low-rank-noise MNIST whose generator is absent from the archive (M018). With those fixed, a focused 6,000-word, seven-figure version built around Figs 6-7 with the image, shunting and measured-response results as its explicit boundary would be a strong Nature Communications paper; in its current form I would return it for focus before sending it to referees.

### What to protect
- The exact conductance-gradient factorization (Eqs 2-3, main.tex 84-102) and the dictionary/capture formalism (106-131): one language for feedback resolution, anatomy and shunting. Do not dilute it during the cut.
- Numerical reproducibility: every headline number in Figs 1-9, R6 and S30/S32 recomputes from Source Data to stated precision with per-run SHA-256 hashes; Table 1 cohort index, prespecified estimands, seed counts and exclusion logs (main.tex 523-552, 1132-1169). Keep all of it, even where text is cut.
- The centered-cut proposition, isospectral matching-vs-quartet design, constructive depth program and the 256-noisy-query estimate-then-learn pipeline (main.tex 284-302; SI note:centered_cut_theorem). This is the paper's new, testable idea; present it as the core, with the hierarchical-Tucker lineage cited (M060).
- The DFA between-by-within factorial (S30), strict-scalar ±0.5 pp equivalence control, and the Fig 2D/E 'large distal residual, no accuracy benefit' result: the cleanest boundary result in the paper.
- Retained negative and failed outcomes reported with the same precision as positives: prospective utility selector lost 20/20 (S35), adaptive reliability shunting worse than no shunt, H4 D4 falsification, tree-Haar excess failing 47-cell replication, Boolean credit contrast failing its 0.01 margin, exact path below neuron-specific on CIFAR-10. Keep this discipline while deleting the per-paragraph hedging prose (M025).
- Fig 4 (branch selection) as drawn: task, three rules, analytic boundary, dose-response, controls and boundary test legible from the artwork alone; the mean-field χ_c derivation and the unimodal-around-chance crossing at χ_c are real.
- Fig 7's in-panel separation of the independent focal-shunt target (C) from the route-generated positive control (D), and the modeled/capacity-not-use claim boundaries (main.tex 317, 331, 335, 991, 421).
- Figure craft: single canvas, captions 212-331 words, every panel letter reconciled with caption and text callouts, interval definitions in eight of nine captions, per-seed hairlines and native-unit contrasts (Fig 2B/C/E/G, Fig 5H, Fig 9C).
- Statistical discipline: declared inferential unit per cohort, nesting never counted as replication, exact sign-flip enumeration at small n, prespecified margins, retrospective analyses labeled as such.
- The Intro's explicit definition of 'local rule' (main.tex 59) and the Francioni reanalysis kept as motivation plus one SI section rather than as a finding.

### Themes
- **One thesis, half the length: restructure around Figs 6-7 with image/shunt/measured-response results as the explicit boundary** [text-only] — The abstract is a twelve-result inventory with no 'here we show'; Results are ten equal-weight sections (R6 has no figure, R5 spends 518 words on a self-described modest effect, R7 is 1,183 words for a scalar toy model), Methods are ~2.7x guidance and restate Results, and the Discussion walks Results order with one negation per 50 words. Referees will not be able to name the advance, and the title's 'dictionary' conflates two senses (route dictionary vs multi-affine feature basis) while the strongest new results involve no route dictionary at all. **Action:** Write the one-sentence answer (morphology fixes a sparse, mostly coarse spatial-credit dictionary; cross-subtree interactions, not task rank, determine which groupings and depths a task needs; image tasks and measured cortical responses use only the coarsest pattern) and build abstract, Introduction close and Discussion opening around it. Cut main text to ~5,800-6,000 words and 7 figures: R3 synthetic screens (3B-F, H) and R6 to SI, R5 to one paragraph folded into Fig 4, R7 split and trimmed to ~700 words with Boolean/fixed-pool/linear-control paragraphs as one-sentence SI pointers. Reduce Methods to one what/how/n/estimand paragraph per Results subsection plus Statistics (~3,000-4,000 words), moving score chronology and fallback history to SI. Rewrite Discussion as four paragraphs (answer; what cut-rank adds over tensor/kernel analyses and predicts for real arbors; biological boundary with the line-423 experiment given an expected sign; limitations) and delete negations of claims never raised. Reserve 'dictionary' for route dictionaries; consider a title that asserts a constraint rather than an identity. (findings: M001, M002, M016, M017, M023, M024, M025, M026, M040, M047, M054, M058, M059, M083)
- **Fig 7 anatomy: fix the broadcast confound in the independent-field test and unify the capture estimand** [existing-data-analysis] — The K=1 depth bin in Fig 7C is literally a constant vector (soma broadcast, 96% of the rank-1 oracle in all 8 cells); depth bins at every K span it while subtree/random/shuffle never do. With a budget-matched [constant, K-1 routes] dictionary subtrees beat depth bins at K=2,4,8,16 (7/8, 8/8, 8/8, 8/8), reversing main.tex 329/341 and Table tab:reciprocal_capacity. Separately, the Pinky 'capture' values (0.902 etc.) are 1-weighted-residual-norm, not Eq. (reconstructioncapture) energy capture (0.979 etc.), so the second-mouse paragraph and S27C are not comparable to Tables S25-S26 and Fig 7C-F; the 47-cell and Pinky replications and the E/F efficiency headline cover only the route-generated positive control (independent-field ratios are 11.4x/2.0x/2.3x, not 14.2x/2.8x/5.3x); the 'independent operator' is itself piecewise constant on the ancestry partition. **Action:** Recompute Fig 7C with the constant column added to every family (or on the centered field, as the tree-Haar analysis already does), report the broadcast share, and rewrite 329/341 and the table conclusion. Pick one estimand (energy capture recommended) for Pinky, 47-cell, S27 and 7G; rename the CSV column. Report E/F on the independent field first (or both) and quote the independent ratios at 331. Reword 'independent' to 'generated by cable physics, not fitted to the routes' and state the ancestry-piecewise-constant structure. Fix the 73.0% sentence (within-pilot, 8 cells, 71.5% inhibitory subset) and give a proxy-precision estimate under a stated prior. Optionally run the reciprocal focal-shunt operator on the 47-cell cohort (the focal solver already ran on 45) so the strongest structural test is not confined to eight proxy-labeled cells; otherwise reword 333, 340 and 421. (findings: M069, M010, M070, M009, M072, M068, M071, M120, M121, M152)
- **Fig 8 shunting: replace 'reshaping vs diagonal gain' with the exact passive identity and reframe the electrotonic boundary as loss of selectivity** [existing-data-analysis] — In the passive reciprocal-cable model the shunt's effect on every descendant adjoint is the common scalar 1/(1+κ_k r_k) and on every other primary subtree another common scalar, i.e. exactly a diagonal gain on the ancestry-route partition (reproduced from source data to 1e-13). The paper's repeated contrast (main.tex 353, 521; SI 518, 1046) is therefore a false dichotomy for the model computed. At unit input-normalized dose the descendant gradient is attenuated ~0.55 log units at every R_m; what R_m changes is off-route sparing (−0.05 vs −0.35), so 'sharply limited the effect' and 'principally in high-conductance states' misdescribe a selectivity result. Fig 8F's relative-local dose is ~0.05 nS at R_m=15,000 vs ~3.6 nS at R_m=300 (50-fold covariation, undisclosed). The active ensemble uses densities of 0.025-0.10x leak in a 30x-leak membrane, reproduces the passive curve to 0.7%, and its parameters are promised in SI S9 but absent. **Action:** Rewrite §R9 title, 353, 360-365, 521 and Discussion 421 around the closed form (descendant factor 1/(1+κr_k); Λ_k ≈ log(1+κr_k) − |log(1−κr_kρ_k/(1+κr_k))| + driving-force correction) and state that this turns Fig 8 into direct support for the route-dictionary thesis. Add signed descendant and off-route changes to Fig 8F/S11, state absolute nS dose per regime in caption F and Table S28, and quote the fixed-nS contrasts at 360. Report channel densities, gating forms and active/passive Jacobian-diagonal fractions in an SI table; describe the ensemble as 'weak steady-state perturbations of a few per cent of the passive operator' or rerun at densities of order the leak (10x passes gates; 30x does not). Cite Rall/Koch-Poggio-Torre on-path inhibition at 65/353. Sharpen the proposed experiment at 423 to the ratio of plasticity change below vs beyond the site. (findings: M012, M011, M013, M073, M074, M075, M122, M123, M124, M125, M126)
- **Fig 9 measured responses: the null is geometric, not biological, as currently constructed** [existing-data-analysis] — The exact compartment-error field at the evaluated checkpoints is 98.4% scalar output error times the fixed pre-training transfer profile (update match 0.976), so Fig 9B and the update-match rows of 9C measure how much of that anatomical vector each four-route span retains (Spearman 0.94-0.98 with retained energy). The 'subtree' dictionary selects thin distal segments (mean 1.31 sites per route, 6/13 scans one-hot, ~48% site coverage), which Fig 9A misrepresents as four disjoint two-site bands with full coverage. The shuffle contrast rests on a 7-target bootstrap that Wilcoxon (P=0.109), sign-flip and t-intervals do not support; all frozen rules lose to treeless ridge; Fig 9E is a by-construction identity and 9F is a data-free icon; the Francioni table carries one-sided P values at the n=6 floor. **Action:** Retitle and reframe R10 around the structure-function partial-correlation test; state that >95% of the exact field is the fixed transfer profile; report route support size, site coverage and retained-energy fractions beside Table S9; redraw Fig 9A to show the actual single-site routes or rerun with routes constrained to ≥2 descendant sites (every scan has ≥3 candidates); report exact Wilcoxon P and positive-target counts beside each 9C interval and soften 394; add one sentence and a 9B reference line for the ridge baseline; move 9D/E to SI and replace 9F with prose; add a same-pipeline positive control with synthetic subtree-confined targets or state the minimum detectable effect; drop the Francioni P values and label the rung 'published data'. (findings: M014, M015, M076, M077, M078, M079, M127, M128, M129, M130, M131)
- **Physical depth (R6): disclose unconverged aligned-D3 fits, the split optimizer recipe and generator constants** [new-experiment] — Every aligned-D3 fit behind the 30.9-, 10.96- and 13.47-point contrasts ran to the 180-epoch cap with validation loss still falling, while main.tex 885 says 'validation stopping within 180 epochs' and no best_epoch is recorded; the optimizer 'interaction' therefore cannot be distinguished from a training-speed effect between LR 0.003 and a bundled 0.0008/split recipe. The main text reports only differences, never the cell means that show exact-path LocalCA (0.887) sits below BP-optimizer soma broadcast (0.912). Line 276 omits that shared-soma LocalCA still improved at D4 (+5.27 pp, 10/10). Several cohort constants and the split TopK rate are undisclosed, and the local-ratio family also changes baselines. **Action:** State at 885 and in SI that aligned-D3 fits reached the budget in every seed; add best_epoch and last-10-epoch slope to Source Data; run a longer-budget check (~50 fits, e.g. 600 epochs or slope stopping) for the five aligned-D3 arms and, if feasible, the one-factor diagnostic (soma-broadcast autograd at 0.0008 unsplit and 0.003 split); until then qualify 274 and 900 as 'at a fixed 180-epoch budget' and replace 'explain why' with 'locate the difference in the recipe'. Give the 2x2 cell means and the 3.09 contrast, all generator constants and batch/split sizes, the full split LocalCA recipe, and rewrite 276 to distinguish BP saturation from LocalCA improvement at D4. Decide whether R6 gets a compact main figure (S31 C/F-G/H) or moves to SI with one sentence. (findings: M007, M055, M056, M057, M110, M111)
- **Signal-noise theory and ancestry (R3/R5): present the retrospective checks and the designed K=4 location honestly** [text-only] — With Σ_route=0 the Fig 3G utility reduces exactly to ‖μ‖²cos₊²/(2L), a per-seed transform of the initial cosine, and the bare cosine predicts both endpoints better (0.991/0.960); the pooled n=540 contains 205 duplicate rows. The 'utility predicts K=4' score outputs K=4 in every seed (13/20 is the base rate) and was redefined after an archived version predicted K=8; the same utility lost 20/20 as a prospective morphology selector (S35) but is called a 'linear control'. The ancestry K-profile is fixed by generator coefficients (−3.05, −0.05, +0.85, 1), so a positive contrast could only appear at K=4, yet the location is presented as discovered and the below-chance accuracies are unexplained. The 1.27-pp contrast carries three adjusted P values. The common-exact-state cosine in Methods masks that the own-state boundary at trained states coincides with χ_c to three decimals (a stronger result). The deranged control fails at χ=0 (−25 pp), not only 'at high conflict'. **Action:** At 192 and Methods 757 state the cosine reduction and P1≈cos relation, report the cosine baseline, condition-mean and within-budget correlations, and describe 3G as an initial-alignment consistency check. Add one sentence at 306 giving the prospective selector's 20/20 loss with the fixed-tree regret and rename 'linear control' to 'original prospective test'. Give the four net route coefficients in R5 and the Fig 5 caption, label K=1 anti-aligned/K=2 null/K=4 aligned/K=8 tie, explain sign-inverted convergence, and say 13/20 equals the constant-prediction base rate and that an earlier score chose K=8 (regenerate or mark superseded source_data/interior_optimum). Declare one primary K=4 test and print it identically in text, Fig 5H and Table S2. Quote own-state cosines as primary at 663 and state that derangement fails at every dose (add the curve to 4D). Note the K=1 laminar shortfall, fix the Fig 3 ribbon caption and add the no-attenuation series or its zero expectation to 3F. Cite the descent lemma at Eq. 6. (findings: M004, M005, M041, M050, M051, M049, M048, M042, M038, M039, M044, M037, M102, M103, M105, M107, M109)
- **R7 morphology framing: cite the tree-tensor-network origin, explain the 6E/6F contrast, and calibrate the grouping and estimator claims** [existing-data-analysis] — The unconstrained cut-rank representability statement and the singular-value-tail bound are standard hierarchical-Tucker results (Hackbusch & Kühn 2009; Grasedyck 2010) never cited; only the centered refinement is new. Root broadcast fails on multi-affine trees (6E) but matches exact credit in the conductance model (6F) because two different rules are compared (unit sensitivity vs calibrated positive profile) and the sign-varying path multiplier mechanism is never assembled. The 'grouping' primary contrasts are near-predetermined by the proven 8/15 floor (observed 0.5632 vs floor-implied 0.5324). The estimator's advantage over a 'short-training baseline' holds only against a two-sweep pilot; a converged pilot ties at equal label budget. 'Require depth three' is vacuous for eight leaves. Panel F's 3%-of-variance residual is framed as categorical. **Action:** State the tree-tensor-network identification and cite the HT sources, presenting only the centered refinement as new. State that the two broadcast rules differ and either add a calibrated-broadcast arm to the multi-affine runs (cheap) or present the sign-varying vs always-positive path-gain mechanism with the parity caveat. Describe grouping outcomes as attainment relative to the proven bound (draw the 8/15 floor in S44E; report learned/bound ratio ~2.2 for 6F) and reserve confirmatory language for the credit contrast. Add a converged-pilot comparison to S41 and replace 'short-training baselines' with 'a two-sweep pilot' if the estimator only ties. Reword 'require depth three' as minimum leaf-count depth with nested targets needing one further level. Add one caption/Methods sentence on per-panel NMSE denominators and noise floors; retitle 6F; state the conductance multiplicity policy; fix the cohort conflation at 857; unify quartet/quartic. (findings: M060, M059, M064, M062, M063, M061, M065, M066, M067, M080, M112, M113, M114, M115, M116, M117, M118, M119, M141)

Rewrite 143 with the CIFAR-10 reversal and the Bicknell & Häusser delta. Add a DFA card to Fig 2A/F and an inset or ×15 tag to 2G. Add the MNIST hyperparameters and 48k/12k/10k split to Methods 568-578. (findings: M033, M034, M030, M029, M031, M032, M035, M036, M081, M082, M099, M100, M101)

### Figure by figure

| Figure | Verdict | Top change |
|---|---|---|
| Fig 1 (exact gradients, dictionary, capture) | Sound and well-anchored; the E/I factorization and capture schematic carry the framework. Panel D's ~0.2-in strips are illegible and the capture definition in the caption (mean per-example projected energy) does not match what the script computes (mean over fields of per-field ratios, zero-norm fields excluded). | Drop or move the 1D strips to SI and reword the capture definition at caption 120/131 to the per-field mean actually computed (M098, M145); this figure is also where to declare the paper-wide semantic palette (M143). |
| Fig 2 (image-task feedback ladder) | Numbers reproduce exactly and D/E is a clean boundary result; but panel C silently uses a superseded cohort, the C rung is strict-scalar under another name, the DFA between-neuron factor (a headline claim) is invisible in the artwork, and G's stacked strips differ 15-fold in scale. | Recompute the panel-C cosines on the frozen-ladder neuron-specific checkpoints (or state the July cohort in caption, Methods 608 and Table S16) and relabel the C tick; add a DFA card to A/F and an inset/×15 tag to G (M033, M034, M035, M036). |

Only the deranged control is misrepresented as failing 'at high conflict' when it is already −25 pp at χ=0, and χ_c(B) is drawn three times. | Add the deranged curve to panel D (or a χ=0 column to E) and state in 228 that derangement fails at every dose; remove panel C to widen D (M048, M106, M105). |
| Fig 5 (ancestry at intermediate budget) | Numerically exact and honest about the small effect, but 55% of the area is schematic, panel I's axis is set by the 61-pp derangement already shown in F, H prints a Holm-Wilcoxon P that differs from the text's sign-flip P, and the K=4 location is presented as discovered when the generator coefficients (−3.05, −0.05, +0.85, 1) make it the only possible positive budget. | State the four net route coefficients and one declared primary test in the caption and H, drop the deranged row from I (or break the axis) and shrink B-D; consider folding E/H into Fig 4 (M050, M051, M052). |
| Fig 6 (interaction structure and tree selection) | The paper's constructive core and its numbers recompute exactly; craft is good. Weaknesses are labeling: D/E/F share an axis label 'test NMSE' with different denominators and floors (F's floor is zero), F's title frames a 3%-of-variance residual as categorical, D has no floor line, 'quartet/quartic' alternate, and the two 'broadcast' rules in E and F differ without saying so. | Add one caption sentence on per-panel denominators and noise floors, draw the 0.0225 floor in D, retitle F ('wrong grouping leaves an irreducible residual') with the 0.013 analytic bound stated, and note that E and F use different broadcast rules (M065, M066, M064, M117, M112). |
